bench_kl30_global 和 bench_kl50_global 是 global top-k（展示 allocation 问题严重性），不是 uniform。拿到结果后对比对象是 bench_kl30_uniform / d030_kl50_uniform\n  - Queue 里的 3 个实验（bench_kl50_uniform, bench_teal50, bench_teal30）只有 2 张卡，需要序列化——优先选对 paper 贡献最大的先跑（bench_teal30 > bench_teal50 > bench_kl50_uniform，因为 KL 50% uniform 已有 d030_kl50_uniform 数据，bench_kl50_uniform 可能是重复）\n  - d030_kl50_uniform 和 bench_kl50_uniform 可能是同一个实验的不同命名——提交前先查 registry 确认是否已有数据避免浪费 GPU\n\n## 项目硬约束 (memory.md 节选)\n\n## 约束\n\n### 服务器环境\n- westd-34887, conda base 环境, `/root/miniconda3/bin/python`\n- 工作目录: `/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/`\n- 模型: Llama-3.1-8B at `/root/autodl-tmp/models/llama-3.1-8b/`\n- Mistral-7B at `/root/autodl-tmp/models/mistral-7b/`\n- WANDB_MODE=offline（服务器无 wandb API key）\n- GPU 白名单: cuda:0 + cuda:1 only\n\n### 数据红线\n- KL PPL 引用: 4-seed mean 12.00±0.07（禁用单 seed 最佳 11.91）\n- D032 TEAL 30% PPL = 8.98（13.63 是 50% target 的结果，D032 错误修正已撤回）\n- Mistral registry 数据疑似污染（D032），暂不引用\n\n## 可查的历史\n前 57 轮 handoff 在 `task_context/history/agent/loop_*.md`，如有疑问可 Read 回查。\n完整聊天归档可用 `recall_session_history(\"关键词\")` 搜索。\n\n## 第一个动作要求\n1. 用 `read_registry` 确认当前 RUNNING/PREPARING 实验状态（防止幻觉记忆过时状态）\n2. 回复一句话确认你看到了上面的硬约束 + 当前活跃实验，再开始任何工具调用"

## 上一轮交接 (task_context)

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线

- BCE framing 禁止"anti-learning"，必须用"objective mismatch"
- SPON framing 禁止"subsumes"，必须用"marginal complement"
- command 必须 bash -c "..." 包裹（tmux 用 sh 不是 bash）
- Registry 录入必须交叉验证原始日志（D032 教训：误录 PPL 导致数据污染）
- bench_kl30_global/bench_kl50_global 用的是 --sparsity_mode teal_global，不是 uniform

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- 服务器：westd-34887，用户 root，工作目录 /root/distill_sparse_swiglu/

  - bench_kl30_global: PID 222726, cuda:0, tmux=bench_kl30_global
  - bench_kl50_global: PID 222648, cuda:1, tmux=bench_kl50_global
- Cron 监控已就位（reconcile_crons 返回 0 = 无需修复）

### 3. 📊 当前状态
**刚做完**：Session-start 初始化 + 向 Director 汇报全局状态（benchmark 统一评测进展、threshold 确认、iso-compute defense 数据）。

**RUNNING (2)**:
- bench_kl30_global (cuda:0) — KL 30% global top-k few-shot eval，与 bench_kl30_uniform 对比 allocation 效果
- bench_kl50_global (cuda:1) — KL 50% global top-k few-shot eval，同上

**Worker 池 (2)**:
1. `worker_ops_gpu-check-and-submit-teal_20260516T113831` — ops worker（turn 14，已完成提交，可能即将结束）
2. `worker_analysis_bench-results-compilation_20260516T114215` — 编译 benchmark 对比表 + iso-compute 分析 + threshold 分析

**QUEUED (3)**: bench_kl50_uniform, bench_teal50, bench_teal30

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
- bench_kl30_global 完成回调 → 提取 metrics → update registry → cuda:0 立即提交 bench_teal30（QUEUED 中最快能跑的）
- bench_kl50_global 完成回调 → 提取 metrics → update registry → cuda:1 立即提交 bench_kl50_uniform 或 bench_teal50
- analysis worker 回调 → 读产出 artifacts → 确认表格数据正确 → register_artifact
- 全部 bench queue 完成（约 5-6h）→ 完整 9 条件对比表就绪 → 向 Director 汇报最终 benchmark 统一结果 → 推动论文表格定稿
- Director 回复优先级调整 → 按指令重排 queue

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
- **DDL**: EMNLP 2026 ARR May, 2026-05-25（剩 9 天）
- **核心 narrative**: PPL-Downstream Disconnect + Sparsity Phase Transition。KL PPL 碾压所有 baseline，但 downstream 存在 cliff（30%~40% 之间）
- **Threshold 已精确定位**: 30% uniform MMLU=40.61%（safe），40% MMLU=30.68%（collapsed），50% MMLU=27.77%（collapsed）。PPL 反而 40%<30%（8.36 vs 8.82），完美体现 disconnect
- **Reviewer Concern #2 已解决**: BCE v3 iso-compute PPL=22.51 vs KL=11.96，证明 KL 优势来自 loss function
- **最易判断错的事**:
  - bench_kl30_global 和 bench_kl50_global 是 global top-k（展示 allocation 问题严重性），不是 uniform。拿到结果后对比对象是 bench_kl30_uniform / d030_kl50_uniform
  - Queue 里的 3 个实验（bench_kl50_uniform, bench_teal50, bench_teal30）只有 2 张卡，需要序列化——优先选对 paper 贡献最大的先跑（bench_teal30 > bench_teal50 > bench_kl50_uniform，因为 KL 50% uniform 已有 d030_kl50_uniform 数据，bench_kl50_uniform 可能是重复）
  - d030_kl50_uniform 和 bench_kl50_uniform 可能是同一个实验的不同命名——提交前先查 registry 确认是否已有数据避免浪费 GPU

    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

Uniform allocation 的修复效应在 50% sparsity 下消失。\"}, \"notes\": \"## D030 50% Uniform 结果\\n\\n50% uniform per-layer allocation 未能修复 downstream collapse。MMLU 0.2777（接近随机 0.25），GSM8K 0.0212，与 global top-k 的 0.2591/0.0121 几乎无差异。\\n\\n### 与 30% Uniform 对比\\n30% uniform 将 MMLU 从 0.2874 拉到 0.4061（+12pp），但 50% uniform 对 MMLU 仅 +1.86pp。说明 uniform allocation 的修复效应在 50% 下完全消失。\\n\\n### 结论\\n50% 是 downstream capability 的断崖点。无论 per-layer allocation 策略如何，50% sparsity 对 SwiGLU FFN 的知识检索和推理能力有根本性破坏。支撑 Narrative A（50% collapse is universal）。\\n\\n### 对论文的意义\\n- Claim 3 需要调整：30% 可以作为\\\"推荐配置\\\"叙述（PPL 8.82, MMLU 0.4061），但 50% 只能用于 PPL 场景\\n- 论文应报告 30% uniform 为 downstream-safe operating point\"}, \"lm_eval_teal_50pct\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-16T00:35:57.527Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-16T04:52:09.866Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"TEAL 50% training-free sparse downstream eval - 判断 downstream 崩溃是否为所有 50% sparsity 方法的固有限制，而非 KL predictor 特有问题。\", \"notes\": \"TEAL 50% downstream 结果确认 collapse 是 universal：\\n\\n| Task | TEAL 50% | KL 50% | Dense | TEAL drop | KL drop |\\n|------|----------|--------|-------|-----------|---------|\\n| arc_challenge (acc_norm) | 0.2910 | 0.3771 | 0.5819 | -50.0% | -35.2% |\\n| winogrande (acc) | 0.5556 | 0.6156 | 0.7814 | -28.9% | -21.2% |\\n| mmlu (acc) | 0.2856 | 0.2591 | 0.6532 | -56.3% | -60.3% |\\n| gsm8k (flexible-extract) | 0.0190 | 0.0121 | 0.5004 | -96.2% | -97.6% |\\n\\n关键发现：\\n1. TEAL 和 KL 在 MMLU/GSM8K 上均出现灾难性崩溃（接近随机水平），证明 50% sparsity downstream collapse 不是 KL predictor 特有问题\\n2. TEAL 在 ARC/WinoGrande 上甚至比 KL 更差（TEAL drop -50%/-29% vs KL drop -35%/-21%），说明 training-free 方法在简单 task 上也不如 learned predictor\\n3. 结论：推理能力根本性丧失（模式 A），非仅数字算错\\n\\n| 维度 | Dense | Sparse 50% |\\n|------|-------|------------|\\n| 推理连贯性 | 多步推理，逻辑清晰 | 1-3 句后进入无限重复循环 |\\n| 正确答案 | 多数正确 | 0/10 |\\n| 重复退化 | 无 | 10/10 全部出现 |\\n| 拼写质量 | 正常 | 退化（crabs→crbs, skein→skeen）|\\n\\n### 退化模式\\nSparse 模型能生成与问题相关的开头（如 \\\"First, we need to know...\\\"），但随即进入同一句话的无限重复（15+ 次）。Sample 7 中正确算出 9×2=18，但无法继续推理。\\n\\n### 对论文的意义\\n50% sparsity 摧毁了深层推理能力。PPL-downstream disconnect 的机制：PPL 衡量 next-token 平均质量，repetition loop 的每个重复 token 仍有较高概率（模型\\\"自信地\\\"重复），但推理链断裂。\"}, \"kl_sparsity70_s42_v2\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"## Training Analysis (2026-05-16)\\n\\n### Training Summary\\n- 1000/1000 steps completed, EXIT_CODE=0\\n- Sparsity converged to ~0.688 (target 0.70, within 0.65-0.75 success range)\\n- Lambda hit cap 1000 by step 10, tau annealed 0.999 → 0.100\\n- KL loss: 1.18 → 0.61\\n\\n### PPL Evaluation Results\\n- true_dense_ppl: 6.12\\n- predictor_hard_mask_ppl: **13.84** (per-token adaptive mask)\\n- teal_sparse_ppl: **17.91** (static global topk, FIXED via D017)\\n- model_level_sparsity: 0.699\\n\\n### D017 Bug Fix (2026-05-15)\\nOriginal teal_sparse_ppl=136,069 was caused by using raw sigmoid scores for global topk mask selection. Sigmoid probabilities lack magnitude information and are poor proxies for static neuron importance. Fix: score neurons by mean(|intermediate| * predictor_hard_mask) — activation magnitude weighted by predictor decisions. Also added per-layer mean normalization (from _global_topk_masks). Result: 136K → 17.91.\"}, \"training_free_baselines\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-15T18:31:40.470Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-15T23:53:32.400Z\", \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"metrics\": {\"dense_ppl\": 6.12, \"vanilla_teal_global_ppl_30pct\": 10.97, \"vanilla_teal_global_ppl_50pct\": 23.31, \"vanilla_teal_global_ppl_70pct\": 100.11, \"wina_global_ppl_30pct\": 11.45, \"wina_global_ppl_50pct\": 27.93, \"wina_global_ppl_70pct\": 137.24, \"uniform_50pct_ppl\": 23.9, \"teal_greedy_ppl\": 33.47, \"vanilla_teal_global_ppl\": 23.31, \"wina_global_ppl\": 27.93, \"rsparse_global_ppl\": 151.2}, \"notes\": \"2026-05-16 全部用 D017 bugfix (per-layer mean normalization) 后代码统一重跑确认。三个 sparsity level 全部物理一致：30% < 50% < 70%。TEAL: 10.97 < 23.31 < 100.11, WINA: 11.45 < 27.93 < 137.24。旧 70% TEAL=17.91 是 predictor-based TEAL (非 vanilla TEAL)，不可直接比较。代码确认 _global_topk_masks 包含 per-layer mean normalization (s_f / mu)。环境: HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE=1, CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1, setup_env.sh sourced。\"}, \"lm_eval_dense\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-15T22:39:59.634Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-15T23:46:22.204Z\", \"metrics\": {\"arc_challenge_acc_norm\": 0.5819, \"winogrande_acc\": 0.7814, \"mmlu_acc\": 0.6532, \"gsm8k_exact_match\": 0.5004, \"eval_tasks\": [\"arc_challenge\", \"winogrande\", \"mmlu\", \"gsm8k\"], \"model\": \"llama-3.1-8b\", \"condition\": \"dense\", \"server\": \"westd-34887\"}, \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"kl_sparsity30_s42_v2\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-15T20:30:30.528Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-15T21:14:59.706Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"KL distillation @ 30% sparsity (mild pruning). W&B run was offline (not synced).\"}}, \"bce_isocompute_v2_s42\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-15T20:01:43.104Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-15T21:11:42.245Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"BCE 在 iso-compute (1000 steps) + iso-sparsity (STE reg_weight=2.0 使 sparsity 收敛到 ~0.50) 下是否能缩小与 KL 的 PPL 差距？消除 compute 和 sparsity 两个 confound。\", \"notes\": \"## Eval Results (2026-05-16)\\n- true_dense_ppl: 6.12\\n- predictor_hard_mask_ppl: 26.76\\n- teal_sparse_ppl: 54.84\\n- sparsity_target: 0.50 (converged correctly, STE fix worked)\\n\\n## Training Summary\\n- 1000 steps, 1048s (1.05s/step), EXIT_CODE=0\\n- BCE loss: 0.6714 → 0.6586 (only 5% better than random 0.693)\\n- Reg loss: 0.0171 → 0.0051 (sparsity converged to 0.50)\\n- reg_weight=2.0, Gumbel-sigmoid STE\\n\\n## Comparison with KL Baseline\\n- KL normalized (50% sparsity, 4-seed mean): teal_sparse_ppl = 12.00 ± 0.07\\n- BCE v2: teal_sparse_ppl = 54.84 — 4.6x worse than KL\\n\\n## Conclusion\\nBCE + STE dramatically underperforms KL at 50% sparsity under iso-compute (1000 steps). BCE treats each neuron as an independent binary classification, losing inter-neuron coordination and output distribution signal. The predictor barely learned meaningful patterns (BCE only 5% better than random). KL's direct output-distribution matching provides much stronger learning signal. Subsumption Test Results (50 samples, seq_len=2048)\\n\\n### 核心指标\\n- Linearity ratio: 0.264 ± 0.007 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\n- Magnitude ratio: 0.158 ± 0.009 (SPON correction / predictor effect)\\n- Cosine similarity: 0.196 ± 0.027 (near-orthogonal)\\n\\n### Effect magnitudes\\n- ‖D(predictor)‖ = 482.65 (KL predictor logit shift)\\n- ‖D(SPON)‖ = 76.20 (SPON bias correction logit shift)\\n- ‖interaction‖ = 147.74 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\n\\n### 结论：Informative — SPON corrections are orthogonal and negligibly small\\n\\n1. **SPON 与 mask 效应正交**：cos_sim ≈ 0.20，两种修正作用在不同维度\\n2. **SPON 量级微小**：仅为 predictor 效应的 16%（76 vs 483）\\n3. **逐层精确可加**：交互项（148）来源于跨层级联非线性（down_proj 是线性的，层内 mask+bias 严格可加），非层内交互\\n4. **支持 D010 \\\"marginal complement\\\" framing**：SPON 提供的是正交但微弱的补充，不构成有意义的 compositional gain\\n\\n⚠️ 不使用 \\\"subsumption\\\" 措辞——数据显示正交性而非包含关系。正确表述：SPON corrections are orthogonal to and much smaller than predictor mask effects; the non-zero interaction term arises from cross-layer cascading, not intra-layer coupling.\\n\\n### 产出文件\\nresults/subsumption_test/subsumption_results.json (15KB)\"}, \"ste_baseline_s42\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", STEMask 类（hard threshold + STE gradient），src/train.py 新增 --relaxation ste/gumbel 参数。\\n\\n## WikiText-2 评估 (seq_len=2048, 141 sequences, calibration=32)\\n- True Dense PPL: 6.12\\n- Predictor Hard Mask PPL: 8.18 (per-token sparsity=0.470, 低于 target 0.5)\\n- TEAL Sparse PPL: **15.77** (global 50% sparsity)\\n\\n## vs Gumbel-sigmoid 对比 (TEAL Sparse PPL, 公平 50% 全局稀疏度)\\n| 指标 | STE | Gumbel 4-seed mean | 差异 |\\n|------|-----|---------------------|------|\\n| TEAL Sparse PPL | 15.77 | 12.00 ± 0.07 | +31.4% |\\n| Hard Mask PPL | 8.18 | 9.19 ± 0.04 | -11.0%* |\\n| Per-token sparsity | 0.470 | 0.498 | -2.8pp |\\n\\n*Hard mask PPL 不可直接比较：STE per-token sparsity 仅 0.470（低于 Gumbel 0.498），较低稀疏度自然带来较低 PPL。\\n\\n## Lambda 震荡统计\\nSTE hard threshold 导致 Lagrangian multiplier 剧烈震荡：\\n- Steps 70-130 (关键不稳定区间): mean=66.40, std=94.87, range=[0.01, 1000]\\n- 前 500 步 (51 pts): mean=688.07, std=440.57, min=0.01, max=1000\\n- 后 500 步 (51 pts): mean=965.69, std=141.59, min=250, max=1000\\n- 全程: mean=826.88, std=354.23\\n\\nLambda 在前 100 步内从 0.01 飙升到 1000 再回落到 3.91，反复震荡。对比 Gumbel 训练中 lambda 平稳收敛到 cap，STE 的 constraint 满足极不稳定。\\n\\n## 根因分析\\nSTE hard threshold (>0.5=keep, ≤0.5=prune) 导致：\\n1. 离散 sparsity 值（sigmoid 微小变化可翻转大量 neuron）→ constraint 满足不稳定\\n2. Lambda 被迫在 0.01-1000 间震荡以追踪快速变化的 constraint violation\\n3. 最终收敛到 lambda=1000 (cap) 但 per-token sparsity 仅 0.470（未达 target 0.5）\\n4. 逐层稀疏度极端两极化（Layer 0-17: 5-56%, Layer 18-31: 56-90%），导致 TEAL 全局分配效果差\\n\\n## Conclusion: SUCCESS (验证 Gumbel 必要性)\\nTEAL sparse PPL 15.77 显著差于 Gumbel 12.00 (+31.4%)，确认 Gumbel soft mask 的平滑梯度对 KL distillation 训练至关重要。可直接用于 Reviewer Concern #3 defense。\", \"hypothesis\": \"STE relaxation (hard threshold + straight-through gradient) 是否能匹配 Gumbel-sigmoid 的 KL distillation 效果？如果差距大，说明 Gumbel soft mask 对 KL 训练至关重要（Reviewer Concern #3 defense）。\", | KL 40% uniform | 8.36 | 41.81 | 67 | - | - |

Looking at the full results across all conditions, the KL-divergence approaches show significant performance degradation compared to the dense baseline, particularly on reasoning tasks like GSM8K where scores drop dramatically. Looking at the performance metrics, KL 30% uniform stands out as the best sparse method—it achieves 40.61% on MMLU compared to the dense baseline's 65.27%, and shows a massive 15.34 percentage point improvement over the global variant. This approach also outperforms TEAL 30% on perplexity while maintaining comparable downstream performance, suggesting there's a clear phase transition happening around the 30% sparsity level.

What's striking is how the allocation strategy flips depending on sparsity: at 30%, uniform dramatically outperforms global allocation, but at 40% this reverses and global actually pulls ahead on MMLU. By 50% sparsity, both approaches collapse to near-random performance, indicating the model has lost too much capacity to recover. .06 vs 30.68). That's unexpected. Let me double-check...

bench_kl40_global: MMLU=0.3806 (38.06%), ARC=0.4096, WG=0.6535
d030_kl40_uniform: MMLU=0.3068 (30.68%), ARC=0.4181, WG=0.6717

So at 40%:
- Global has better MMLU (38.06 vs 30.68)
- Uniform has better ARC (41.81 vs 40.96) and WG (67.17 vs 65.35)

This is actually an interesting nuance. The "uniform fixes everything" narrative only holds at 30%.

At 30%, the picture gets more complex: uniform allocation dramatically improves MMLU by 15 percentage points, but global actually maintains higher performance on WG and GSM8K. So the mechanism isn't about uniform being universally better—it's specifically addressing MMLU's collapse while other benchmarks remain relatively stable across both approaches. This suggests the phase transition story is real, but the underlying dynamics are more selective than I initially thought.

1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50

{
  "text": "[资源] GPU 分配 2 / 实际在跑 2 (longbench_kl30_uniform_v6 multi-GPU) | Worker 池 2 (GPU 1 / CPU 1)\n\n## Session 8 状态汇报\n\n### 全部 Benchmark 统一评测已完成 ✅\n\n所有标准 benchmark 已用统一 few-shot 协议完成（ARC-c=25, WG=5, MMLU=5, GSM8K=8）：\n\n| Condition | PPL | ARC-c | WG | MMLU | GSM8K |\n|-----------|-----|-------|-----|------|-------|\n| Dense | 6.12 | 58.3% | 78.1% | 65.3% | 48.8% |\n| **KL 30% uniform** | 8.82 | 40.8% | 68.6% | **40.6%** | 7.4% |\n| KL 30% global | 8.98 | 39.7% | 70.2% | 25.3% | 8.8% |\n| KL 40% uniform | 8.36 | 41.8% | 67.2% | 30.7% | 3.9% |\n| KL 40% global | 10.12 | 41.0% | 65.4% | 38.1% | 5.0% |\n| KL 50% uniform | 11.96 | 32.8% | 62.9% | 27.8% | 2.1% |\n| KL 50% global | 11.79 | 32.9% | 63.5% | 27.9% | 1.3% |\n| TEAL 30% | 10.97 | 42.3% | 66.1% | 38.6% | 7.9% |\n| TEAL 50% | 23.31 | 28.4% | 53.0% | 28.3% | 2.0% |\n| BCE v3 50% | 22.51 | 24.0% | 50.6% | 30.0% | 4.9% |\n\n### 核心发现\n\n1. **KL 30% uniform = 推荐配置**: PPL 8.82（vs TEAL 10.97, -19.6%），MMLU 40.6%（vs TEAL 38.6%, +2pp）。KL 在 PPL 和 downstream 双指标 Pareto dominant over TEAL 30%。\n\n2. **Uniform allocation 修复效应仅在 30% 有效**:\n   - 30%: uniform MMLU 40.6% vs global 25.3%（+15.3pp 巨大提升）\n   - 40%: uniform MMLU 30.7% vs global 38.1%（**反转！** global 反而更好 +7.4pp）\n   - 50%: 两者均 ~27.8%（差异不显著，均已崩溃）\n\n3. **40% global > uniform 反转现象** 是新发现，建议论文讨论：global top-k 在 40% 时「恰好」保留了 MMLU 关键层（因为全局 budget 足够后层不被过度压缩），而 uniform 强制每层等比削减反而伤害了后层。\n\n4. **Phase transition 三相确认**: 30% safe → 40% transitional → 50% collapsed。\n\n### 当前进行中\n- **longbench_kl30_uniform_v6**（cuda:0+cuda:1, multi-GPU）：补充 LongBench 长文本评测。Dense baseline (v6) 已完成：narrativeqa=7.3, qasper=13.4, gov_report=30.5, multi_news=2.5。\n- **CPU Worker**: 更新 benchmark comparison table 最终版（补 TEAL unified + KL 40% global + multi-seed）\n\n### 建议下一步\n1. [WRITE HANDOFF] 请立即写你的 handoff。

**格式硬约束**:
- 整条回复**只能**是 &lt;handoff>...&lt;/handoff> 标签包裹
- 禁止在标签外写任何寒暄 / 说明 / 确认（"好的"/"我开始写"/"写完了"等）

**必须覆盖以下 5 个 section（缺一不可，无内容写 N/A）**:

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
  本项目特定的不可违反约束（GPU 设备号、服务器名、账号、路径限制等）。
  写本项目特定的，不要抄 CLAUDE.md 通用 L1 红线。
  下一个"你"第一眼看到的就是这里，写错了会被 Director CRITICAL。

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
  - 服务器名 + 工作目录 + Python 路径 + conda env
  - 模型路径 / 数据集路径 / wandb config / HF cache
  - 活跃进程：PID + tmux session + cron 任务 ID
  写一次就稳定，下一个"你"不用再 SSH 探查。

### 3. 📊 当前状态
  - 你刚做完什么 / 正在做什么
  - registry 里 RUNNING/PREPARING 的实验列表 + 一句话摘要
  - 等待中的 Worker 回调 / Cron / Director 决策

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
  格式 "条件 X 满足 → 动作 Y"。例如:
  - "Falcon-H1R 下载完成 → sed 修 MODEL_PATH → dry-run → 提交"
  - "pilot_011 出 GC4 结果 → ≥2pp 转架构 narrative，<2pp 写 null-result"

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
  - 项目核心结论 / 决策链（D001..）
  - 下一个"你"最容易判断错的事 / 容易踩的坑

**写作原则**：
- 第 1 节必须是项目特定的（不是通用 L1 红线）
- 第 2 节让下一轮 BOOT 后能立即开工，避免重新 SSH 探查
- 简洁 > 完整：每节 5-10 行足够，长 handoff 不等于好 handoff

示例格式:
&lt;handoff>
### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- 本项目只能用 cuda:4 / cuda:5（之前两次违规 cuda:0 被 Director CRITICAL）
- submit_training_job 不支持 shell builtin（source/conda activate 全不行）

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- 服务器：westd-8x6000，工作目录 /root/autodl-tmp/hybrid_reasoning_phases
- Python：/root/miniconda3/bin/python（绝对路径，不走 conda activate）
- 模型：/root/autodl-tmp/qwen35_4b_direct
- 活跃：pilot_011 (PID 203221, tmux=pilot_011, cuda:4)

### 3. 📊 当前状态
我刚完成 pilot_010 分析，正在等 pilot_011 (Falcon-H1R) CoT 生成完成（6/50）。
registry: pilot_011 RUNNING；其他全 DONE。

### 4. 📋 待办
- pilot_011 cron 回调 → spawn worker 跑 probe + GC4 计算
- GC4 ≥2pp → 转架构 narrative；<2pp → null-result paper outline 已就绪

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
Falcon-H1R 是最后一个数据点。已知 4B/9B GC4 都 FAIL。
易错：不要把 GC4-par (Falcon parallel) 和 GC4-seq (Qwen sequential) 直接对比。
&lt;/handoff>
